  arr JSON_ARRAY_T; out_arr JSON_ARRAY_T;
  obj JSON_OBJECT_T; out_obj JSON_OBJECT_T;
  keys JSON_KEY_LIST;
  out_bool BOOLEAN; out_number NUMBER; out_text VARCHAR2(32767);
  out_date DATE; out_timestamp TIMESTAMP;
  c CLOB; out_clob CLOB; b BLOB; out_blob BLOB; begin out_timestamp:=elem_timestamp.TO_TIMESTAMP(); end;
